It’s not like anything terrible happened, but that’s when I noticed all this starting, I guess.\n\nDoctor: When you think about how you've been feeling, does it seem mostly connected to the changes at work?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think mostly, yes. So, yeah, I guess it is starting to affect my job more than I’d like to admit.\n\nDoctor: Have you been avoiding food because you’re worried about your weight or body shape?\n\nPatient: No, not because of weight or how I look or anything like that. So, yeah, I guess it is starting to affect my job more than I’d like to admit.\n\nDoctor: Have you been avoiding food because you’re worried about your weight or body shape?\n\nPatient: No, not because of weight or how I look or anything like that.
